## `ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `ARG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Load Scale Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Freight Car Overload/Unbalanced Load & Wheel Load Gauge
- **Intro:** Load Scale Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Freight Car Overload/Unbalanced Load & Wheel Load Gauge — Verify Scale System Isolation and LOTO Status: Electrical isolation: 0 VAC at the load cell excitation terminals (measured with a CAT III multimeter). Exclusion zone: 3.0 m radius…
- **Key tolerance:** Electrical isolation: 0 VAC at the load cell excitation terminals (measured with a CAT III multimeter). Exclusion zone: 3.0 m radius from scale pit edge.
- **Standards:** `IRAM 35051:2018`, `Resolution 2019`, `ISO 7503-1:2016`, `R 106-1:2011`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/ARG-4912-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)

- **Role:** Hazmat-Derailment Safety Officer

- **Role:** ECP-Brake-Rigging Mechanic
